Trigger on any task involving init.lua, plugin/*.lua, lsp/*.lua, vim.pack.add(), vim.lsp.enable(), or "native neovim config" — even if the user just says "add a plugin" or "configure LSP" in a native-style config. ([fredrikaverpil/dotfiles](https://github.com/fredrikaverpil/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/stow/shared/.claude/skills/nvim-config) · ★261)
